## 40. CPU physical Touch gating for native fused detections — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R04; pure CPU core, no full-match publication yet.** Added `rust/volley-core/src/touch_pipeline.rs`, which interprets source-selected RAVEL/VballNet ball geometry together with the *provisional* CPU Track ID boxes and RAVEL Touch probabilities. The algorithm combines native existing moving-average RAVEL Touch peak extraction, near-player IoU association, actual ball reach, and gravity-corrected pre/post velocity discontinuity through `volley_core::touch` and `volley_core::motion`. Unlike proximity-only crossing rules, straight flight alone is not a Touch. Mixed VballNet/RAVEL ball source inside the ±8-frame motion interval suppresses rather than fabricates a velocity break. All geometry is normalized by video dimension and supplied as pixel values to the existing motion thresholds. Contact method is `unknown_touch`; athlete identity is explicitly unverified and no serve/attack/block is invented.

- The interface `evaluate_block` accepts ≤1200 contiguous evidence frames with output bounds for a future bounded streaming CPU job; defaults are Touch threshold 0.50, smoothing five frames, 10-frame separation, reach 0.35 of box height, and 0.35-second physical contact interval. Does not decode video or invoke ONNX. Synthetic tests cover actual direction reversal, straight-line flight, unreachable actor, ball-model source switching, malformed frame order, and disjoint emission windows. `cargo fmt --all` and `cargo test -p volley-core --offline touch_pipeline` (5 tests passed). CPU replay JSONL integration, source integrity checks, and final precision/recall evaluation remain pending.

## 41. Full native CPU motion-gated Touch proposal replay — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R04; physical Touch *proposals*, not final validated player statistics.** `rust/volley-app/src/cpu_touches.rs` adds `POST /api/cpu/native/touches` with project/video/existing `replay_id` and optional `settings` (`threshold`, `smooth`, `min_gap`, `max_reach_ratio`, `min_interval_sec`) plus `GET /api/cpu/native/touches?project_id=...&touch_id=...` to inspect its manifest. Reads the immutable `native_cpu_replays/<replay_id>/tracked_frames.jsonl` from the same verified project and prepared source; rejects missing/mismatched project/video, symlinks, invalid metadata, truncated/out-of-order frames, unsupported 30-FPS setup and SHA-256 mismatches before publishing. The per-project archive lock remains held during processing. Only isolated `<VOLLEY_RUST_DATA_ROOT>/projects/<id>/native_cpu_touch_runs/<touch_id>/` receives a new `touch_candidates.jsonl` and SHA-256 manifest. GPU caches, original videos, prior CPU tracking snapshots and Python Studio remain untouched. A change to Touch thresholds creates a separate version without ONNX, FFmpeg or GPU work.

- Streaming CPU replay uses 240-frame core chunks with 40-frame symmetric halos. It calls the existing `volley_core::touch_pipeline::evaluate_block` to combine RAVEL Touch peak smoothing, provisional tracked-player boxes, ball reach and physically measured ball velocity change; it deduplicates proposed events across block boundaries. All observed geometry is converted from normalized boxes to **actual prepared-video pixel dimensions** before the pre/post motion check. A model-source transition within ±8 frames suppresses a spurious ball jump. `touch_candidates.jsonl` stores `unknown_touch` with measured direction/speed/residual and an optional **provisional** Track ID. Manifest explicitly marks `contacts_authoritatively_confirmed:false`, `athlete_identities_verified:false`, and `cpu_processing_complete:false`.
- WSL `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo test --workspace --offline` (105 passed, two optional ONNX tests ignored) and `cargo check --workspace --offline` passed. `cargo test -p volley-app --offline cpu_touches` tests bounded replay, integrity/truncation, a synthetic reversal versus straight flight, emission across 240/480-frame chunk seams without duplicate contacts, and a stricter CPU Touch-threshold resnapshot that rejects an earlier accepted proposal. Explicit RTX 5070 Ti CUDA end-to-end smoke also passed: native VballNet + RAVEL sessions, full synthetic video GPU inference, frame fusion, CPU Track IDs and CPU Touch replay; synthetic recording has **zero** physically supported contacts, as expected. Real-match action classification, rally grouping, end-user UI, quantifiable accuracy comparison and final production switch remain pending.

## 42. Streaming ball-supported provisional rally reconstruction — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R04/R05: core stage, not authoritative rally labels or integration with montage.** Added `rust/volley-core/src/rally_reconstruction.rs` with deterministic bounded-memory `RallySegmenter` and validated CPU-only `RallySettings`. It consumes frame-ordered selected-ball center/score observations and the existing motion-gated RAVEL Touch *candidates*. The conservative defaults tolerate up to 75 missing ball frames, split after a 60-frame stationary interval, cap touchless segments after 180 frames, require at least 45 frames, 12 observed ball frames, 25% coverage, and **two** motion-supported Touch candidates. All thresholds are replayable without changing GPU inference. A provisional proposal records detected start/end, number and span of Touch candidates, ball coverage, provisional Track IDs, missing-identity counts, and the boundary reason; it explicitly denies verified serve, athlete identity, and authoritative game classification. It does not invent attack/serve/point outcome or cut source footage as final montage data.

- Six Rust core tests verify that short ball-visibility holes do not split one play, long holes produce distinct segments, motionless ball terminates a segment before idle frames, continuous ball visibility does not merge separate plays across a touchless pause, one candidate Touch is not enough to claim a provisional rally, and invalid parameters/frame order/Touch probabilities fail closed. `cargo test -p volley-core --offline rally_reconstruction` and `cargo check --workspace --offline` passed. Pairing this classifier with persisted CPU Track ID and Touch JSONL with SHA-256 is the next part of the stage; full real-match comparison is deferred under the migration plan.

## 43. Native CPU rally snapshot linked to verified Track ID and Touch inputs — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R05: provisional rally evidence, not final classified events or montage.** Added `rust/volley-app/src/cpu_rallies.rs` and `POST /api/cpu/native/rallies` with explicit `project_id`, `video`, `replay_id`, `touch_id`, optional `settings`; inspect result via `GET /api/cpu/native/rallies?project_id=...&rally_run_id=...`. The native input parser checks selected project and video, prepared-video 30-FPS/dimensions and size/mtime, both versioned manifests (including original Track ID source SHA-256 embedded in the Touch source manifest), symlinks, expected frame count and Touch candidate count, strictly increasing Touch frames, 512-KiB maximum JSONL source line, and the full SHA-256 of **both** immutable evidence files. Runs under the per-project archive guard, streams frame by frame without loading the match into memory, then atomically publishes a *new* `<VOLLEY_RUST_DATA_ROOT>/projects/<id>/native_cpu_rallies/<rally_run_id>/rally_candidates.jsonl` and SHA-256 `manifest.json` if verification succeeds. Prior CPU, GPU and Python results are unchanged.

- Each candidate contains exact detected frame boundaries, ball observation coverage, observed Touch count and first/last Touch frames, provisional Track ID audit, boundary reason, and a deterministic candidate ID derived from the chosen replay/Touch source version and boundaries. `authoritative_rally:false`, `serve_verified:false`, `athlete_identities_verified:false`, `montage_ready:false` and `cpu_processing_complete:false` prevent falsely treating raw proposal geometry as finished Studio gameplay or ready-for-montage clips. No arbitrary technique/point/score is inferred. Changing the strictly validated CPU thresholds creates an independent replay snapshot without GPU, FFmpeg or ONNX.
- Tests: `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo test --workspace --offline` (**114 passed, two CUDA/ONNX integration tests ignored in ordinary workspace run**), and `cargo check --workspace --offline` passed; dedicated app tests cover linked 2-rally positive fixture, threshold resnapshot, corrupted hashes, out-of-order Touch records, undeclared extra source rows and truncated input. Explicit `scripts/ci/smoke_rust_ort.sh <installed ORT 1.31.1> cuda full` passed on NVIDIA RTX 5070 Ti with native model execution and 18-frame synthetic video through **GPU raw evidence → fusion → CPU Track IDs → motion-gated Touch → provisional rally snapshot**. No contact or rally is invented from that synthetic clip. Full real-game boundary precision, final serve/technique classifier, gold marks, montage scene compatibility and product UI remain pending per the user's rewrite-first plan.

## 44. Conservative event evidence labels and safe provisional clip boundaries — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R05 — pure Rust CPU core, no automatic serve/attack/score inference.** Added `rust/volley-core/src/event_evidence.rs`: a strict `MotionInput` interpreter that transforms saved motion-supported `unknown_touch` measurements into *physical* evidence labels (`direction_reversal`, `direction_change`, `direction_and_speed_change`, `measured_acceleration`, `measured_deceleration`, `motion_residual_only`). It never claims a serve, attack, block, dig, verified player identity, or scored rally. Native `bounded_clips` takes every sorted nonoverlapping provisional rally interval and an optional context margin up to 180 frames, keeps every detected frame intact, clips context to video bounds and midpoint-safe spaces between neighboring rallies, and rejects overlapping, reversed, missing-video or out-of-range intervals. This is safe **preview/manual montage planning** evidence, not automatic final montage eligibility.

- `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo test -p volley-core --offline event_evidence` (4 tests) and `cargo check --workspace --offline` passed. Tests verify kinematic categories without fabricated sports actions, reject invalid probability/motion or fictional Track ID, protect complete detected play while adding context without overlapping neighboring segments, and reject invalid unsorted or out-of-range sequences. Next: version-bound read-only preview and manual montage planning without overriding canonical CPU runs. Original Python runtime/media and `master` are unchanged.

## 45. Verified native rally preview and manual original-source montage route — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R05/R06 — backend API, not a finished UI or trustworthy auto-generated sports highlights.** Added `rust/volley-app/src/native_rally_preview.rs`. `GET /api/cpu/native/rallies/preview?project_id=...&rally_run_id=...&offset=0&limit=24` exposes a bounded (max 80/card page) read-only list of *provisional* rallies, safeguarded candidate IDs, original source video references, full-rally context clips that never overlap or crop detected play, motion-supported Touch counts, and per-Touch **kinematic** evidence. Each Touch is still `unknown_touch`, an actor's Track ID is provisional, and `authoritative_game_events:false` / `automatic_montage_eligible:false` remain explicit. No serve/attack/block labels are invented. The API validates the project/video, prepared-video identity, exact saved rally/Touch version relationship, both JSONL checksums, complete input counts/order, immutable candidate IDs, interval ordering, and underlying original-video timeline before returning any content. Touch preview is limited to 48 events/card while retaining a correct total count; no full-game payload is returned to the browser.

- Added **explicit manual** `POST /api/montage/native/start-from-rally-candidates` with project ID, the exact provisional `rally_run_id`, 1–80 distinct 24-hex `candidate_ids`, optional soundtrack/encoder. It revalidates complete snapshots **server-side** and constructs a set of unmodified, whole-rally `SelectedClip` boundaries, then delegates rendering to existing isolated Rust montage using the **original-resolution source**, not the analysis proxy. Client-supplied arbitrary trim offsets, automatic selection and unreviewed athlete/action attribution are disallowed. Manual clip order follows the explicit selected ID order. Combined duration is limited to 600 s; original file existence/duration is re-probed. This is a manual preview bridge only; canonical legacy CPU run registration, gold marking, full UI interactions and automatic event scoring still require work.
- `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo check --workspace --offline`, `cargo test --workspace --offline` (**122 passed; two optional ONNX/CUDA integration tests ignored**) passed. Pure preview/ID/source tests verify paginated labels and original-media clip geometry, SHA-256 tampering, stale/duplicate/out-of-range IDs, and no automatic eligibility. An explicit NVIDIA RTX 5070 Ti CUDA full-video smoke passed through both model inferencers, raw frame fusion, provisional player tracks, physical Touch candidates, provisional rally snapshot and **read-only rally preview**, yielding zero fictional touches or rallies on a synthetic non-game video. Frontend/UI and real-match quality validation remain pending.

## 46. Rust-served first standalone preview/montage UI, native gold marks and global filtered paging — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R06 — a functioning independently served Rust development UI with a small transitional same-origin JavaScript bridge, NOT a Rust/WASM browser app.** The WSL Rust toolchain currently has only `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu` installed; there is no installed `wasm32-unknown-unknown` target or cached wasm-bindgen/web-sys/Yew dependencies. We did not quietly substitute a fake WASM build or change the original Python UI. The experimental standalone page at `GET /studio/rust` on loopback Rust port `8875` is served by Rust/Axum from `rust/volley-app/static/studio.html`, `studio.css`, `studio.js`, via `rust/volley-app/src/studio_web.rs` with `Content-Security-Policy`, `nosniff`, and no-store headers. It shows available project/isolated CPU-rally run dropdowns, paginated provisional rally cards, RAVEL physical Touch evidence, player provisional IDs, a local prepared-video MP4 viewer (clip-specific seek and stop), multi-page manual selections, original-quality manual montage launch, optional music upload, encoding choice, progress/cancel/download, and gold right-click or star toggle. Browser code sets untrusted values by `textContent` / DOM construction, never `innerHTML`.

- `GET /api/cpu/native/rallies/runs?project_id=...` lists up to 100 well-formed isolated provisional snapshots (bounded/validated run IDs; rejects symlinked/invalid folders), with canonical raw status explicitly unconfirmed. `rust/volley-app/src/native_rally_marks.rs` provides `GET/POST /api/cpu/native/rallies/marks` with exact project and rally-run version scope, a BTreeSet of immutable 24-hex candidate IDs and SHA-256 compare-and-swap `expected_revision` (`absent` on first write). Writes go ONLY to isolated `projects/<id>/native_rally_marks/<rally_run_id>.json` under a project archive lock; the immutable GPU fusion, Track ID, Touch, rally JSONL and legacy gold marks never change. Each set re-verifies the source manifest and exact candidate ID against the verified full run, rejecting foreign/stale IDs and stale revisions. Native preview `GET /api/cpu/native/rallies/preview` now supports **global**, full-run `gold_filter=all|marked|unmarked` with `filtered_candidates` count **before** pagination. Manual montage selection remains explicit 1–80 immutable rally IDs, independent of the visual filter.
- `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo check --workspace --offline`, `cargo test --workspace --offline` (**129 passed, two CUDA/ONNX optional tests ignored in this normal run**), and Node.js `node --check rust/volley-app/static/studio.js` all passed. New regression tests include a full isolated real-MP4 gold bookmark scenario (create, read, idempotent update, conflict on obsolete revision, reject forged candidate, unmark), global gold-filter pagination across three candidates, source checks, and an actual Axum loopback HTTP test serving HTML, JavaScript, CSS and status with strict security headers and no legacy production `/api/health`. Explicit native NVIDIA CUDA smoke (`scripts/ci/smoke_rust_ort.sh ... cuda full`) again passed full video GPU inference, fusion, provisional Track ID, physical Touch, preliminary rally and read-only preview stages.

Remaining: compile a real browser Rust/WASM application after adding supported target/dependencies, add full analysis/profile/player/Quick Reel UI, actual browser interaction and screenshots, ergonomic gold context menu, player identity review, frontend accessibility/device QA, authenticated old-project reopen and final real-match accuracy/parity. Do **not** mark the rewrite complete or switch the default Python production launcher. The new standalone UI is for controlled isolated testing and can show empty runs if no Rust CPU snapshots exist.

## 47. First real Rust/WebAssembly client engine compiled and executed — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R06 (real WASM, not a complete all-Rust frontend).** WSL `rustup target add wasm32-unknown-unknown` eventually completed after the initial install attempts stalled. Created the fifth Rust workspace crate `rust/volley-web/`, a dependency-free native-testable Rust selection state engine compiled to `wasm32-unknown-unknown`. Its exported integer-only ABI and a fixed 24-byte WASM memory buffer implement the authoritative **browser-side** ordered selection of exact lowercase 24-hex rally IDs: add/remove, contains, count, read at index, deduplication, strict validation and hard 80-rally cap. This replaces the JavaScript Map as the source of manual montage selection; JavaScript only stores display labels and handles DOM/HTTP. If the actual WASM cannot load, montage selection is disabled rather than silently falling back to a different implementation.

- Pinned compiled artifact at `rust/volley-app/static/volley_web.wasm` (24,608 bytes), served by Rust/Axum at `GET /studio/rust/selection.wasm` with `application/wasm`, `nosniff`, no-store cache controls, and a restricted Content Security Policy that allows `'wasm-unsafe-eval'` but not arbitrary `'unsafe-eval'`. The original Python browser/server port is not used. `scripts/ci/test_volley_web_wasm.mjs` actually instantiates and executes that exact deployment binary in Node WebAssembly and asserts order, deduplication, capacity, duplicate removal, uppercase rejection and memory exports. Native `cargo test -p volley-web --offline` adds separate unit coverage. A fresh `cargo build -p volley-web --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --release --offline` was compared **byte for byte** with the published `.wasm` via `cmp`.
- `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo check --workspace --offline`, `cargo test --workspace --offline` (**134 passing, two explicitly ignored native ONNX integration tests**), Node JavaScript syntax checks and actual WASM execution all passed. The actual NVIDIA CUDA end-to-end synthetic GPU→fusion→Track ID→Touch→rally→preview smoke passed in this development stage before final WASM wiring; no CUDA logic was changed afterwards. Overall rewrite and real-match acceptance remain unfinished. Important limitation: project, analysis, player tabs and DOM/network control still use a temporary JavaScript bridge, and no WASM framework/complete UI parity is claimed.

## 48. Native Analysis and Players tabs with explicit GPU/CPU version flow — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R06/R07:** Added a native Rust endpoint `GET /api/studio/rust/analysis-overview?project_id=...&video=...` in `rust/volley-app/src/studio_analysis.rs`. It enumerates only the selected project's stored native RAVEL/VballNet model runs, verified-fusion manifest metadata, provisional Track ID replays, motion-gated Touch versions and provisional rally snapshots, bounded to 350 on-disk version folders per stage and 60 displayed entries per stage. It rejects symlinks, foreign video/project ownership, suspicious stage schemas, missing SHA format and incorrect provisional/verified status flags. The response is intentionally `metadata_only:true` / `hashes_not_reverified:true`: the existing processing endpoints still revalidate complete source JSONL SHA-256 checksums on each step. No Python process or legacy source cache is written.

Each action creates its own isolated version. Saved rally run cards link back to the native preview route. The player tab uses the existing Rust library `POST /api/players/library/native` with `read`, `create` and `rename` actions and mandatory SHA-256 compare-and-swap revision on writes. It never silently assigns unconfirmed RAVEL tracks to athletes.

No in-browser DOM screenshot acceptance has been performed. Full player photo/ReID linkage UI, profile edit UI, Quick Reel pipeline, automatic full-run scheduler and final action classification remain outstanding.

## 49. GPU job progress survives Rust Studio page reload — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R07 UX consistency, not an inference change.** `rust/volley-app/src/inference_full.rs` adds read-only `GET /api/inference/native/full/active?project_id=...`: for the **selected project** it reports the current running full-model GPU job ID, video, RAVEL/VballNet model, phase, decoded frames and progress, or explicit `active:false` on idle/completed jobs. It validates project ownership and does not reveal another project's job identifier, video or model. It performs no model loading, GPU work, cache mutation or job scheduling. A focused unit test checks valid in-progress, idle, completed and foreign-project behavior. The existing `/api/inference/native/full/status` and cancellation APIs are unchanged.

- `rust/volley-app/static/studio_tabs.js` reconnects to the existing job after opening/reloading the Analysis tab or changing the current project/video, resumes periodic native status polling, keeps cancel wired to the **same original job ID**, and does not issue a second GPU start. `rust/volley-app/static/studio.js` emits a small `volley:project-ready` event after its async project dropdown fills, fixing an initial-load race when only one project exists. The new state is not persisted in browser storage; the authoritative server job manager remains the single source of truth. After a server process restart, existing persisted interrupted status is still not misrepresented as running.
- Checked `cargo fmt --all`, `cargo check --workspace --offline`, `cargo test --workspace --offline` (**135 passing, two intentionally ignored ONNX integration tests**), offline `cargo build -p volley-web --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --release`, actual `node scripts/ci/test_volley_web_wasm.mjs` execution and `node --check` of both browser scripts. No changes to GPU tensor decoding or algorithmic outputs. Remaining frontend work includes automated browser-session reattachment tests, complete player identity/photo controls, RAVEL touch/ball visualization, project creation UI, and the Quick Reel flow.

## 50. Standalone native provisional Track ID ↔ project player manual registry — 2026-11-08

**PARTIAL R07/R08, backend-only.** The previous Python-compatible `POST /api/players/identity/native` is scoped to legacy `analysis/<run>/players.json` and **cannot** safely bind new `native_cpu_replays/<id>/tracked_frames.jsonl` by itself. Added `rust/volley-app/src/native_player_tracks.rs` and scoped `GET/POST /api/players/native/tracks`: for an explicitly chosen project, video and immutable CPU `replay_id`, the server verifies the prepared-source bytes/mtime, native 30-FPS CPU track manifest status and file ownership, and streams/validates **every** saved frame and the full source JSONL SHA-256. A bounded index of up to 20,000 **provisional** Track IDs exposes first/last observed frame, detected-frame count, strongest candidate frame/score/normalized bounding box. The list is paged (max 100 rows) and never pretends that RAVEL query indices are cross-window athlete identities.

- Only explicit operator actions `assign`, `unassign`, `exclude`, `restore` modify an isolated, versioned sidecar `<VOLLEY_RUST_DATA_ROOT>/projects/<project>/native_track_assignments/<replay_id>.json`; **no GPU/CPU source JSONL, Python project/cache, photo registry, or player models are changed**. Owners must be in the selected project's `player_ids` roster and global Rust athlete registry. One Track ID cannot silently be reassigned to another player, and excluded tracks must be restored before assignment. Each edit requires the previously read SHA-256 `expected_revision` (`absent` for new files), checks action uniqueness, bounds 200 IDs/change, uses a per-project archive guard, rejects symlinks and stale/mismatched source replay fingerprints. Manual assignment metadata is intentionally separate from the still-provisional geometry; no automated ReID/photo validation or action statistics confirmation is asserted. Read requests are also source-verified and do not rerun the GPU.
- `cargo test -p volley-app --offline native_player_tracks` **3/3 passed**, covering hashed replay index and corruption, roster/source-linked documents, and an isolated real-video full edit cycle including stale revisions, reassignment refusal, exclude/restore and unchanged raw source. `cargo check --workspace --offline` passed. Browser-facing player linkage controls and physical track crops are a **separate next stage**.

**PARTIAL R08 UI.** Extended the independent `/studio/rust` **Игроки** tab with an explicit manual Track ID review panel (project video selector, saved `native_cpu_replays` version selector from metadata-only Analysis stage, verified paginated Track ID list, detections count/best frame/score, project-roster athlete picker, multi-ID assign/unassign/exclude/restore, and current assignment/exclusion totals). All untrusted names and file metadata are rendered through DOM `textContent`, no `innerHTML`. The browser sends only numeric Track IDs, explicit operator actions and prior SHA-256 edit revisions to the native Rust API. Neither automatic ReID grouping nor implicit player binding is presented as complete.

- `GET /api/players/native/track-photo?project_id=...&video=...&replay_id=...&track_id=...` creates a **read-only, on-demand JPEG crop** from the selected project's *prepared original* video using the strongest saved CPU player detection. The server first revalidates the exact source video/CPU manifest, every raw tracking JSONL frame, full SHA-256, and requested Track ID. The real `wasm32-unknown-unknown` crate builds offline and deployment WASM executes under `node scripts/ci/test_volley_web_wasm.mjs`; all three browser JavaScript files pass syntax checks. The assignment/preview UI is still a temporary JS bridge; the full DOM/application is not yet implemented in Rust/WASM, and real-match ReID accuracy and saved athlete reference-photo enrollment are pending.

## 52. Duplication audit, canonical ownership map and mandatory no-repeat gate — 2026-11-08

**Architecture correction, not a feature-completion claim.** Inspected the commit history and module/route consumers for prior stages 14–21 versus 38–51. Verified that legacy Python-compatible CPU caches and the new Rust GPU→CPU JSONL pipeline each developed identity assignments, ReID-adjacent paths, rally selection, gold marks and montage routes. The legacy modules remain in use, and their on-disk schemas/IDs differ; deleting them as alleged dead code would break existing-project compatibility. Recorded exact responsibilities, typed data boundaries and ordered unification plan in `docs/RUST_ARCHITECTURE.md` and source-typed ownership inventory in `docs/RUST_CANONICAL_OWNERS.json`.

- Added a fail-closed `scripts/ci/check_rust_ownership.mjs` gate; it checks unique canonical owner/domain paths, source-qualified mutable stores, expected Axum route-to-owner bindings, new undocumented guarded API routes, duplicate route registration and architecture documentation presence. Self-tests deliberately inject a duplicate route, an undocumented player API, a second ReID owner and a colliding state store, and verify each causes a failure. `node scripts/ci/check_rust_ownership.mjs --self-test` passed (**14 owned domains, 34 guarded endpoints, 4 version-specific sidecar paths**). Node is used for CI only, not as a Python/Node runtime dependency for the native application.
- Policy: before code, inspect all Rust sources and `git log` for related work; declare canonical feature owner, legacy/native source formats, immutable evidence, mutable sidecar and all consumers; extend the owner or write a narrowly scoped adapter, never a parallel implementation; test old and new formats, update coverage and migration diary, commit and publish each coherent stage. Progress is assessed by completed end-to-end behaviors and removed parallel authorities, **not by number of commits/stages**. Next stage extracts duplicated source-agnostic revision and sidecar persistence into a shared Rust service without altering existing files.

## 53. Remove duplicated mutable sidecar implementations, preserve legacy/native schemas — 2026-11-08

**Этап 53 / 85 по общей дорожной карте; следующий — 54 / 85.** См. [RUST_ROADMAP.md](RUST_ROADMAP.md) — все предыдущие этапы и дальнейшие конкретные задачи; 85 — пересматриваемый план, не процент готовности.

**Completed safe shared-infrastructure deduplication; domain adapters intentionally remain until the next source-aware unification.** Added `rust/volley-app/src/versioned_store.rs` as the sole Rust implementation for SHA-256 mutable-sidecar revision (`absent` for no document), format checking/CAS, bounded regular-file/symlink-rejecting reads, and size-bounded atomic JSON sidecar writes in guarded direct-child paths. Added regression tests for missing/stale/revisioned files, unsafe symlinks and parents, size limits, exact JSON/sha compatibility and no changes to immutable Track ID evidence. **No changes to the persisted schemas or original media.**

- Replaced repeated revision/read/persist code in **five existing adapters**: `native_player_tracks.rs`, `player_identity.rs`, `reid_group_edits.rs`, `native_rally_marks.rs`, `rally_marks.rs`. Each still validates its own source schema and owns project/feature locking; no cross-version Track ID or gold candidate IDs are silently translated. This is actual common code, rather than just documenting duplicate modules. Direct replacement of legacy modules is still unsafe: `rallies.rs` and `player_montage.rs` depend on their legacy CPU cache and schema. Native photo/embedding extraction and source-aware identity/rally policy unification remain distinct pending tasks, not completed features.

The Windows WSL launcher `scripts/start-volley-rust-dev.ps1 -CheckOnly` now runs the mandatory ownership self-test **before** offline Cargo check, without imposing a Node.js dependency on the actual Rust server runtime. Development check results: full offline Rust workspace tests **143 passed / 2 optional ONNX integration tests ignored**, `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo check --workspace --offline`, and `node scripts/ci/check_rust_ownership.mjs --self-test` (**14 owners, 34 guarded routes**) passed. Real-match parity is deliberately still deferred until the complete rewrite.


- **Полная карта стадий (с запросом пользователя):** `docs/RUST_ROADMAP.json` является единственным источником для 85 записанных/будущих пунктов; `docs/RUST_ROADMAP.md` автоматически генерируется и показывает **последний записанный этап 53 / 85**, следующий **54 / 85** и все будущие задачи с критериями. План 85 — ориентир, его нельзя выдавать за 53/85 готовности приложения. Счётчик обновляется в одном коммите с каждым новым результатом.
- **Проверка реально запускается:** `scripts/start-volley-rust-dev.ps1 -CheckOnly` исправлен для PowerShell 5.1 (вместо небезопасной передачи многострочного `bash -c` используется временный UTF-8/LF Bash-файл и отдельные аргументы); штатная проверка запускает ownership + roadmap + offline Cargo. `scripts/ci/run_checks.py` выполняет эти проверки и в обычном локальном PR-цикле с Node. При первом параллельном тестировании однажды возник временный `409` при втором CPU-пересчёте тестового проекта; отдельно и в последовательном workspace-прогоне все 143 теста прошли. Возможную гонку блокировок между Rust-процессами/WSL файловой системой следует исследовать в приёмочных тестах; ложного утверждения об отсутствии флейков нет.

## 54. Типизированный EvidenceSource и проверка происхождения — 2026-11-08

**Этап 54 / 85 ЗАПИСАН; следующий 55 / 85.** Создан единый тип происхождения анализов в `rust/volley-core/src/evidence_source.rs` без нового формата хранения или параллельного алгоритма идентификации. `EvidenceSource::LegacyCpuCache` содержит идентификаторы проекта, видео, выбранного legacy-запуска, базового GPU-кеша, отметку размера/mtime `players.json` и **явно неизвестный FPS**. SHA-256 legacy-файла не выдумывается и не выдаётся за проверенную контрольную сумму. `EvidenceSource::NativeGpuCpuReplay` привязан к проекту, видео, 32-hex идентификатору версии CPU, SHA-256 **полностью проверенного** `tracked_frames.jsonl`, fingerprint подготовленного видео и конкретному 30/1 FPS. Неизвестная частота кадров, 29.97/60 FPS, некорректный хеш или подменённый source stamp отклоняются для native.

- `ScopedTrackId` включает **весь EvidenceSource и Track ID**. Числовые ID из старого и нового источника, разных run/cache/replay или отличающегося source fingerprint **никогда не равны** и не могут быть объединены по совпадающему числу. Даже десериализованный объект должен пройти `checked()` — один `serde_json::from_value` не считается проверкой происхождения. Контракт сериализуется с явным `source_kind: legacy_cpu_cache | native_gpu_cpu_replay`, source stamps и typed `FrameRate`.
- Старый `rust/volley-app/src/player_identity.rs` и новый `rust/volley-app/src/native_player_tracks.rs` **подключены как source-specific адаптеры**, а не переписаны заново. В ответах существующих read/edit API появились только дополнительные `source_kind` и `evidence_source`; старые поля, revision CAS, сохранённые ссылки, исходные JSON-файлы и FFmpeg/GPU результаты остаются неизменны. Сканирование legacy `players.json` по-прежнему проверяет known Track IDs и stamp, native — точный SHA и manifest. API legacy не начинает принимать native replay ID.
- `docs/RUST_CANONICAL_OWNERS.json` теперь имеет **15** владельцев, включая единственный `evidence_source` в volley-core; два существующих identity-модуля указаны адаптерами. Unit tests проверяют round-trip, ограничения FPS/sha, source stamp, отсутствие смешивания двух идентичных Track ID и невалидную десериализацию; интеграционные тесты сохраняют исходную схему sidecar и проверяют source поля на реальном MP4.

**Ограничения:** наличие типизированного происхождения не означает, что объединена логика ручных назначений или появилось настоящее ReID. Это задачи 55–65. Не подтверждено качество на реальных матчах, поэтому готовность продукта не равна 54/85.

Both the SHA-verified native CPU replay adapter (`rust/volley-app/src/native_player_tracks.rs`) and the existing legacy on-disk cache adapter (`rust/volley-app/src/player_identity.rs`) construct this set from independently validated evidence. The shared contract rejects duplicate IDs, more than 20,000 identities, native ID 0 and cross-run/cross-source scoped identities. ## 57. Перевод старых и нативных API на общий identity-сервис — 2026-11-09

**Single Rust identity mutation policy; both HTTP APIs now share it.** Introduced rust/volley-core/src/identity_review.rs as the single typed ManualReviewState / ReviewAction owner. Both rust/volley-app/src/player_identity.rs (historical project cache JSON) and rust/volley-app/src/native_player_tracks.rs (SHA-verified Rust CPU replay JSON) use that same policy for assign, unassign, exclude and restore operations. Distinct evidence validation, legacy/native persisted schemas, project locks, SHA/revisions, CAS and HTTP fields remain as compatible adapters. docs/RUST_CANONICAL_OWNERS.json now identifies identity_review.rs as the sole business-policy owner.

- Entire batches are preflight-validated before mutation: reject duplicated, unknown or out-of-scope Track IDs, missing athletes in the roster, excluded assignments, silent reassignment conflicts, and invalid restore. Legacy allow_reassign remains explicit; native assignments are always non-overwriting. Excluding removes the owner; restoration only changes visibility. Legacy rejected automatic proposals remain rejected until an explicit assignment.
- Old legacy cache records for Track IDs outside the current inference, unrelated groups and metadata survive actions on other IDs. No original video, legacy Python cache or original saved project was modified; no Python runtime exists in this active branch.
- Verification: WSL cargo fmt/check and full workspace tests passed: 153 successful Rust tests and two optional model-dependent tests intentionally ignored. Regression tests cover both source adapters, all-or-nothing changes, stale/missing data, native revision conflicts, real synthetic-MP4 file-backed edits and legacy saved history preservation. Ownership guard passed.

**Not finished:** image ReID embedding creation, shared confirmed-athlete consumers, manual reference photo enrollment, full web UI or real match accuracy evaluation. Stage counter is not product readiness.
## 58. Проверка совпадений и конфликтов визуальных ReID предложений — 2026-11-09

**No physically impossible or contradictorily assigned saved manual group may re-enter an automatic ReID preview.** Extended the single canonical Rust ReID owner, rust/volley-core/src/reid.rs, with ManualGroupMember and valid_manual_group. It enforces distinct IDs, bounded membership, source frame-range validity, zero co-visibility and no contradictory manually confirmed owners. Existing OpenVINO cosine similarity, conservative complete-link groups and explicit unconfirmed suggestion status remain unchanged.

- The legacy JSON group-edit adapter now reuses the canonical compatibility check instead of maintaining independent overlap/owner rules. Its manual merge remains explicitly operator-driven and versioned; conflicting IDs fail as a single action.
- The saved-group restore adapter now vets every previously pinned manual group **before** suppressing automatic candidates. Invalid, duplicated, conflicting, overlapping or stale-member groups are skipped entirely rather than published as safe candidate groups. One malformed group cannot preempt a later valid group by claiming its IDs. No saved legacy sidecar is rewritten during a read.
- Added read-only invalid_manual_groups_ignored count to ReID preview for visibility. The existing score_type explicitly labels cosine similarity as similarity, not probability or confirmed athlete identity. The athlete assignment pipeline is still separate and only human-reviewed actions become authoritative.
- **Verification:** WSL cargo fmt, cargo check and full offline workspace tests passed: 156 successful tests, two optional ONNX model tests ignored. New unit tests cover overlapping saved groups at inclusive frame bounds, conflicting confirmed owners, duplicate ID reuse across groups, exclusion/stale tracks and preservation of valid manual suggestions. Existing native/legacy group-edit tests passed. No new model inference occurred, and real-match ReID precision was not measured.

**Next:** the same reviewed athlete decisions must be exposed to statistics and original-media montage without using two independent sources of truth (stage 59).
## 59. Общие подтверждённые identity решения для статистики и монтажа — 2026-11-09

**Only explicit athlete-confirmed Track ID assignments can credit a real person in statistics or personalized montage.** The canonical rust/volley-core/src/identity_review.rs now owns ReviewedLink and resolve_reviewed. It resolves only source-known, manually reviewed, non-excluded IDs whose athletes still belong to the selected project roster; conflicting confirmed owners invalidate an ID instead of crediting either athlete.

- The isolated native CPU replay player adapter uses this same reviewed-only resolver for displayed owner fields. The historical JSON cache statistics adapter also uses the resolver. Both statistics and personalized montage call this one adapter for the same input CPU snapshot and hence receive identical reviewed identity decisions. Neither API grants a player identity based on ReID group similarity, a manually merged *suggestion* group, or co-visible visual matches.
- Removed implicit inheritance of an athlete name across a saved manual ReID group from the historical statistics adapter, including its previous dangerous one-confirmed-member propagation. Removed unrelated ReID group file reads, stamp gating and revision checks from both statistics and montage: changes to visual candidate grouping alone no longer invalidate real confirmed player counts or prevent montage. Stale GPU player evidence, manual assignment changes, CPU event updates and roster changes still invalidate the personalized montage as before.
- Versioned statistical algorithm upgraded to volley-native-player-statistics-v2-reviewed-links-only, so old cached aggregates cannot silently be reused as the new semantics. Stored raw evidence, old identity documents, original videos and both sidecar schemas remain untouched.
- **Tests:** WSL cargo fmt, cargo check and full offline workspace tests passed: 159 tests successful, 2 optional ONNX tests ignored. Regression tests verify no implicit identity inheritance from saved ReID groups, conflicting manual owners ignored, unreviewed references never credited, explicit native track owners displayed from the same core resolver, and existing original-video personalized montage semantics. Canonical business logic is shared; legacy and native data remain separate source scopes.

**Still incomplete:** the native GPU/CPU provisional replay does not yet contain full authoritative sports actions; therefore native personal Touch/attack totals and automatic Quick Reel remain pending rather than fabricating event labels. The next stages cover embedding creation, photo enrollment and real-match evaluation.
## 60. Извлечение ReID embeddings из новых native Track ID — 2026-11-09

Real native OpenVINO C ABI ReID extraction, without a Python runtime, is implemented in rust/volley-inference/src/reid_openvino.rs. The sole model is the bundled ov/person-reidentification-retail-0287.xml/.bin. A verified source-frame bounding box is cropped with FFmpeg to BGR24 128x256; Rust converts it to 1x3x256x128 f32 CHW (raw 0–255). Real OpenVINO CPU inference produces a finite, nonzero 256-float embedding normalized to unit L2 length. A SHA-256 model fingerprint includes both XML and BIN contents as well as the exact BGR preprocessing contract.

- New POST /api/players/native/embeddings is source-scoped, read-only and limited to 1–8 unique Track IDs. It checks explicit expected replay SHA, the original verified CPU replay, current prepared video, crop geometry, the original source/replay metadata before and after inference, and both model files before and after inference. Decodes have a 35-second timeout. Results include vector, model fingerprint and provisional/no-auto-assign markers; they do not modify original videos, caches or athlete assignments.
- The Windows Rust launcher can accept -OpenvinoLibrary with an absolute WSL .so path and sets the native loader environment. No Python process, OpenVINO Python API or old Studio app is invoked. The existing installed native OpenVINO shared library under the HOME-PC WSL distribution was used for test only; no machine-specific paths are committed.
- Canonical ownership ledger includes the native ReID endpoint and the OpenVINO inference adapter. Model-specific on-disk embedding persistence and manually reviewed photo enrollment remain future work (stages 61–62).
- Verification: actual OpenVINO model produced repeatable 256-float normalized embedding from two inferences, and the SHA-verified native replay → FFmpeg synthetic video crop → real OpenVINO endpoint integration test passed including stale-source rejection. BGR order, dimensionality, zero/nonfinite rejection and model fingerprint invalidation have unit coverage. cargo fmt/check/test workspace offline, WASM selection runtime, and ownership self-tests passed: 163 tests passed, two optional ONNX integration tests ignored, 35 guarded API routes. No real-match ReID accuracy claim or GPU OpenVINO benchmark is made.

## 61. Сохранение фотоэталонов игрока с проверкой источника — 2026-11-09

Implemented global, isolated, reversible native athlete photo reference enrollment. There is no Python fallback, model re-inference or mutation of existing project media. New Rust endpoints:
- POST /api/players/native/references: action read/enroll/undo. Enroll only if the selected project roster includes that athlete and the exact, SHA-verified native CPU replay Track ID has already been manually linked to that athlete. No ReID suggestion, unassigned ID, excluded ID, other project's Track ID or stale replay can create an enrollment. Requests use an optimistic revision CAS; each athlete's global album has an exclusive native lock.
- GET /api/players/native/reference-photo: returns a bounded, SHA-256-verified JPEG only when the reference ID is still present in the selected athlete's album. No user-provided paths are dereferenced. Symlinked album directories, lock or image are rejected. Reading never writes.
- Store schema volley-native-player-references/1: players/native_reference_photos/<player_id>/index.json under the isolated Rust data root and content-addressed ref-<id>.jpg files. JPEG content SHA-256 and project/video/replay/source SHA/Track ID provenance are preserved. 64 photos/player and <=3 MB/photo; all photos have manually_enrolled=true. The album belongs to a global athlete, for future cross-match reuse, while assignments remain project/replay-specific.
- Undo first atomically removes the versioned index entry, then deletes its JPEG, ensuring it cannot be used again for matching. Stale revisions or repeated undo fail closed. The original GPU evidence, CPU replay, source JPEGs and previous legacy albums remain untouched.

**Tests 2026-11-09:** full synthetic match lifecycle: no enrollment before manual ID review, manual assignment, SHA-replay verification, FFmpeg JPEG cropping, saved bytes and content hash, subsequent photo GET, rejected stale SHA and duplicate/stale revision, explicit undo and rejection of removed images. Schema tampering tests reject duplicates, unsafe filenames and non-manually-enrolled entries. Offline cargo fmt/check/test workspace passed: 164 Rust tests successful, two optional ONNX integration tests ignored; native OpenVINO real inference, Rust/WASM execution and 37-route ownership negative self-tests also passed.

Next: stage 62 must add fingerprint-specific native embedding caches, with explicit invalidation on model and source change. A stored photo is not by itself an embedding or an automatically verified athlete.
## 62. Версионирование модели embeddings и инвалидирование кеша — 2026-11-09

- Native read-through vector cache at rust/volley-app/src/native_reid_cache.rs, in isolated projects/<project_id>/native_reid_cache/<replay_id>.json. Each vector is scoped by verified replay SHA256 and the OpenVINO model fingerprint. Cache records pin the encoder name, preprocessing ID, dimension and finite unit-length vectors. File and track count limits, symlink rejection, exclusive lock and atomic JSON write are enforced.
- Native player-tracks endpoint uses cache hits without decoding video or running the encoder and invokes native OpenVINO only for missing vectors. Model or replay changes invalidate the full cached embedding space. The source video, replay and model are checked before committing new vectors. The HTTP response reports cache hits/misses without asserting athlete identities.
- Verified: Rust workspace offline cargo fmt/check/test passed (167 successful Rust tests, 2 model tests ignored). Synthetic-video native OpenVINO test exercises repeat cache-hit and source SHA rejection; isolated cache tests exercise model/replay invalidation, invalid vectors, merges and symlink rejection. Whole-match GPU accuracy is not claimed.

Next stage: use existing canonical Rust cosine ReID groups with verified native embeddings; proposals remain unconfirmed.

- Read-only POST /api/players/native/reid-groups runs the single canonical Rust volley-core/src/reid.rs::build_groups algorithm over only verified native CPU replay Track IDs whose vectors are already available in the model-fingerprint-checked isolated cache. No Python, OpenVINO session, FFmpeg decode or GPU operation is invoked for similarity reads.

The evidence adapter checks source checksum/manifests and on-disk reviewed assignments; the normalized appearance embeddings remain unconfirmed. Manual excluded IDs and distinct confirmed roster owners are hard vetoes; frame overlap blocks merges; missing embeddings remain unmatched. A model fingerprint change refuses stale vectors.

The JSON response reports similarity_type=cosine_similarity_not_probability, confirmed=false and auto_assign_enabled=false. No existing sidecar or user data is changed by preview.

Added native fixture checks for high-similarity non-overlapping tracks, confirmed different owners, overlapping tracks and out-of-scope cached IDs, and no-vector unmatched tracks. Existing core complete-link and ambiguity regression tests continue to pass. Real-match precision and interactive acceptance remain pending.

## 67. Единая траектория мяча и анализ экстремумов изменения движения — 2026-11-09

- Physical-motion parameters in rust/volley-core/src/motion.rs are now canonical TOUCH_MOTION_CONFIG, reused by rust/volley-core/src/touch.rs; there is no separately tuned extremum model. The bounded ball_motion_extrema algorithm extracts local, gravity-compensated direction/speed discontinuities from a single selected-ball trajectory, rejects nonfinite/duplicate/out-of-order frames, and never claims player identity or confirmed contact.
- POST /api/cpu/native/ball-extrema reads one explicitly selected, SHA256-verified and 30-FPS native CPU replay. It validates all frame indices and provisional status, refuses source-model switches inside the diagnostic window (RAVEL vs VballNet), uses original media dimensions, and examines at most 1200 selected-ball frames. Output records ball source, frame range, observed samples, physical motion extrema and explicit gpu_inference_rerun=false and touches_confirmed=false. Source evidence and saved analyses remain read-only.
- The Players review panel now offers a bounded ball-motion diagnostic with start-frame and frame-count fields and an explicit warning: a ball motion change alone is not proof of a touch or a game action. Core synthetic trajectories distinguish reversal from straight and gravity-only flight.
- Verified: 180 offline Rust workspace tests passed with 4 test threads; 2 optional model tests ignored; Node JS syntax and canonical ownership guard (41 routes) passed. Source replay SHA mismatch and unsupported source tests passed. Real-match annotation/precision benchmarks remain pending.

Next: tie physical Touch outputs to verified tracking/rally source stamps and prevent geometry-only credit.
## 68. Подтверждение физически обоснованных касаний вместо геометрии — 2026-11-09

- The existing native CPU Touch detector already requires RAVEL peaks, real ball-direction/speed change, a reachable player box and a consistent selected-ball source. No overlap with a player bounding box alone can manufacture a motion_supported Touch. Saved Touch events explicitly remain provisional and do not claim verified athlete identity or technique.
- Strengthened the downstream native rally join in rust/volley-app/src/cpu_rallies.rs: each attributed Touch must refer to a real player Track ID observed in the SAME verified CPU source frame, with validated player detection and provisional status; a Touch must also have a selected ball observed in that frame. Missing, fabricated or duplicate tracked IDs, forged verified identity flags and no-ball Touch frames now fail closed even if a modified source has been rehashed. Unattributed motion contacts can still be represented without inventing player identity.
- Snapshot lineage remains replay ID+replay SHA256 -> motion Touch ID+Touch SHA256 -> provisional rally ID. The rally manifest documents physical_touch_player_gate=same_frame_verified_cpu_track, physical_touch_ball_gate=same_frame_selected_ball. This gate intentionally favors correctness over coverage; rare off-frame model contacts require a future explicit evidence-gap handling contract instead of silent over-crediting.
- Verified: 181 offline Rust tests passed with four threads (two hardware/model tests ignored). New adversarial test recomputes SHA after replacing a touched player's ID, deleting the observed ball and forging confirmed identity; all are rejected. ## 72. Классификация подач, атак и блоков по подтверждённым событиям — 2026-11-09

- Added a canonical evidence-only game-action classifier rust/volley-core/src/action_review.rs. Physical motion/Tou​​ch from a SHA-verified native CPU snapshot remains unknown_touch unless a human explicitly reviews an event and records exactly one named action: serve, attack, block, dig, overhead_pass or low_touch. Unreviewed velocity reversals, intersecting player bounding boxes, unverified source hashes and speculative labels cannot produce a named action. Game-action confirmation DOES NOT assert a verified athlete identity.
- Added POST /api/cpu/native/actions/review (read, set, clear). Each action is matched to the exact source Touch frame and provisional Track ID from the selected, source-verified rally/Touch manifest. A full streamed SHA256 verification precedes reading/editing. The isolated, versioned source-bound action sidecar lives at projects/<id>/native_action_reviews/<rally_run_id>.json and pins source_replay_id, source_touch_id, source_rally_sha256 and source_touch_sha256. Changes require CAS expected_revision and a per-project exclusive guard; stale source, duplicate or fabricated contact, label not in the reviewed vocabulary, invalid identity or missing physical evidence fail closed. 1024 manually reviewed actions maximum per run.
- Verified: 193 successful offline Rust tests with four test threads (2 optional native model tests ignored). New source fixture checks explicit attack, SHA mismatch after source tampering, exact Track ID matching, duplicate/forged classification and loss of manual reviewed status; existing unknown_touch fallback and activity guards stay green.
- IMPORTANT LIMIT: this is human-confirmed classification, NOT an automatically trained attack/serve/block neural classifier. The trained inference models presently emit Touch evidence but no independently validated reliable technique logits. Automatic technique accuracy cannot honestly be claimed without new labeled examples and model verification. This stage meets safety acceptance; an automatic game-action classifier remains an explicit quality gap in stage 84.

## 75. Единый рендер/монтаж из оригинала для обоих форматов — 2026-11-09

- Confirmed that BOTH source adapters enter the same Rust FFmpeg export path: legacy CPU run selection through rust/volley-app/src/rallies.rs -> montage::start_native_from_run and native CPU manual rally selection through rust/volley-app/src/native_rally_preview.rs -> montage::start_manual_montage; both call montage::start_native_montage, whose isolated runner delegates all video encoding decisions to rust/volley-media/src/montage.rs::OriginalClip and compose_args. No legacy Python video encoder, analysis proxy or resized cached preview is used for exported pixels. Source paths are validated against the project's original video files.
- Fixed mixed landscape+portrait FFmpeg composition: the original maximum WIDTH and original maximum HEIGHT are now taken independently instead of selecting the size of the single largest-area source. Each extracted video is padded without downscaling or upscaling any source pixel dimensions. The shared output_profile validates FPS and sizes and rejects pathological >32MP composition rather than silently reducing original resolution; output FPS is the highest real source rate rather than forced >=24 FPS. Lossless stream-copy of already encoded equal-format fragments remains preferred when possible; otherwise H.264 CRF15 (CPU) / CQ17 (NVENC) quality policy is preserved.
- The runner now verifies native source width, height, FPS and H.264 codec AFTER each FFmpeg extraction and compares final composed width, height and FPS against the shared source-derived output profile, also after adding soundtrack. Duration verification detects truncated outputs rather than accepting any tiny nonzero video. The saved selection manifest records source native width/height, verified output dimensions/FPS, and the quality policy, and claims original_resolution_verified only after all checks pass. Read-only originals and isolated Rust output folders remain unchanged.
- Added real offline FFmpeg integration tests rust/volley-media/tests/original_montage.rs. Fixtures synthesize separate original 640x360 30fps native and 360x640 25fps legacy streams, apply a nonzero legacy offset, render both from their original videos, verify the composed 640x640 30fps H.264 MP4 with no scaling, sufficient duration, correct source clip frames, and native-source extraction PSNR >30dB; a second fixture tests true stream-copy of two same-resolution H.264 clips. Pure negative cases cover 4K landscape+portrait, oversized canvas, invalid FPS and missing inputs. FFmpeg binary availability is detected and clearly printed if integration fixtures must be skipped; in this environment ALL actual FFmpeg tests ran successfully.
- Verified: 200 successful Rust tests across the workspace (two optional ONNX tests ignored), cargo fmt/check and Rust original-resolution FFmpeg integration tests all passed. The canonical montage/FFmpeg owner is now rust/volley-media/src/montage.rs; app job/API and native/legacy source adapters are explicitly registered. End-to-end audio/vertical fidelity and source-to-final full-frame accounting remain for stages 77 and 84 respectively.

## 77. Вертикальный монтаж, аудиодорожка и проверка качества MP4 — 2026-11-09

- Added explicit, serde-validated VideoLayout::Original (default) / VideoLayout::Vertical to the one shared FFmpeg owner rust/volley-media/src/montage.rs. The legacy CPU rally selection, native manual rally preview, native reviewed-player attack montage, and direct montage route all forward the same layout to the same encoder. Existing clients that do not specify layout continue to export at original native pixel dimensions without scaling or cropping, as in stage75.
- Vertical mode creates a true even-dimension 9:16 H.264 canvas at the maximum original source clip height. It proportionally fits EVERY full input frame using Lanczos and pads borders; it never stretches the athlete/ball or cuts away the volleyball court. The explicit vertical mode may downscale landscape frames to preserve the entire view, so the completed receipt accurately sets original_resolution_verified=false while retaining source_clips_native_resolution_verified=true. Unscaled source clips are always extracted from canonical original project recordings, including offsets for legacy CPU versions; no proxy/inference video frames are substituted.
- Hardened music overlay with source-scoped isolated upload path, selectable 0-100% volume, bounded looping of short soundtracks, AAC 48kHz 192kbps output, 48000 Hz async resample with zero-based PTS, short fade and video stream-copy. Music remains the only final audio (native clip extraction intentionally omits original audio); without a chosen track the final video has no audio stream. The athlete highlight route now accepts the same optional music and volume as the manual route.
- Implemented verify_mp4_quality(path, dimensions/FPS, expected duration, audio_expected) in the canonical exporter: ffprobe must report exactly one H.264 yuv420p video with square SAR, correct output width/height, real average frame rate and checked frame count/duration. Audio-required outputs must contain exactly one AAC stream at a legitimate sample rate with audio/video start <=80ms apart and endpoint skew <=120ms; audio-free outputs may not silently add a soundtrack. Missing/truncated/malformed streams fail closed BEFORE final MP4 is published. Per-job selection.json now stores mp4_quality, selected layout, verified original clips, no-crop policy and separate vertical/original H.264 quality policy.
- Rust Studio manual montage now exposes original/portrait 9:16 format and a music-volume slider; athlete-attack montage has independent layout, music picker and volume controls, with validated isolated audio upload and preview invalidation after format changes. Video elements use object-fit:contain in page/fullscreen to prevent visibly stretched portrait clips.
- Extended real offline FFmpeg integration tests in rust/volley-media/tests/original_montage.rs: generated 640x360 30fps native and 360x640 25fps legacy original inputs, extracted original source intervals (including a nonzero legacy offset), composed two full rallies into 360x640 30fps portrait without crop, applied 0.47-second PCM audio looped to a 2-second AAC video, verified 60 encoded frames and <120ms audio/video end skew, and fully decoded the final MP4 with FFmpeg -xerror. Also verified correct refusal of absent audio, wrong profile, truncated target duration, invalid layout/volume and wrong stream expectations. Node browser contract tests exercise portrait selection, audio upload, volume forwarding, manual overrides and cancellation; live Axum static-asset tests verify availability of the UI controls.
- Verified: 208 successful Rust workspace tests with 4 test threads (two optional ONNX integration tests ignored), cargo fmt/check, all five real FFmpeg tests passed, JavaScript syntax and browser tests passed. This is not a subjective production-quality benchmark; stage84 still needs a real match video comparison.

## 78. Независимый Quick Reel: свежий GPU→CPU→монтаж — 2026-11-09

- Replaced the terminal awaiting_native_inference placeholder in rust/volley-app/src/quick_reel.rs with a real per-run Rust pipeline. Each Quick Reel run has a new random UUID and analysis salt, and proceeds through (1) shared content-addressed video preparation only, (2) TWO freshly created full-video CUDA ONNX jobs for VballNetV4c ball and RAVEL-013 players via inference_full::start, (3) immutable two-SHA model fusion, CPU Track ID rebuild, motion-gated Touch candidates and rally reconstruction via the EXISTING inference_fusion, cpu_retrack, cpu_touches and cpu_rallies implementations, then (4) ranked original-source rally montage with vertical 9:16 H.264 and the user-uploaded isolated AAC soundtrack. GPU and CPU model results are never reused, even if the source video was previously analyzed by a different Quick Reel or a normal project.
- The Quick Reel stage runner lives separately in rust/volley-app/src/quick_reel_pipeline.rs under one canonical quick_reel.rs owner. It executes in a temporary private namespace at VOLLEY_RUST_DATA_ROOT/quick_reels/<uuid>/pipeline/projects/<internal 12hex ID>. This is NOT a real project: the main projects/projects.json, player library and project analysis folders are NEVER touched. Per-source internal aliases allow identical filenames in separate video folders. Each alias resolves to the canonical ORIGINAL path, selected file UUID and original size/nanosecond mtime, authenticated against the durable Quick Reel request. preparation::plan_for accepts the virtual root only if the run directory and source tokens match. It reuses only the same global prepared/ media cache (or already-analysis-ready original); fresh GPU/CPU artifacts remain under this run's isolated pipeline root.
- Existing native CUDA/ONNX model job and CPU algorithms remain authoritative owners; no duplicate tracking, Touch, ball or rally logic was introduced. Quick Reel reserves the shared GPU semaphore, requires full canonical model execution (no silent CPU fallback), records fresh model/fusion/replay/Touch/rally UUIDs and source SHA256, and propagates source changes/errors/cancel signals. For the reel itself, unreviewed provisional rallies are ranked ONLY by motion-supported physical contact evidence and ball coverage; do not misrepresent them as automatically verified attacks, players, serves or authoritative rally labels. A lack of qualifying rallies >=3 seconds fails explicitly without a fabricated output.
- Original media boundaries come from the full SHA-verified native rally and Touch streams via native_rally_preview::verified_manual_clips; a 600-second total bound and maximum 12 selected rallies apply. The shared stage77 encoder verifies native original clips, H.264, output 9:16 dimensions and AAC audio sync. Only a successful verified MP4 is copied to Quick Reel's own quick_reel.mp4, with result.json provenance including actual encoded rally keys, new per-video GPU/CPU source IDs and hashes, original source selection and MP4 quality; the temporary internal project is then reclaimed. Missing, failed or cancelled stages leave output:null. Stage/status now has four stages: preparation, GPU, CPU and montage, with per-video progress and preserved failure stage. The full browser file picker/progress/download UI belongs to stage79.
- Tests include real synthetic FFmpeg source video and audio, private workspace generation, prepared-media reuse, cross-run and substituted source rejection, proof that real user projects are untouched, actual attempt to launch the fresh GPU inference worker, accurate model-missing failure with no made-up MP4, early cancel, and deterministic motion-only ranking. Verified 211 passing offline Rust workspace tests (2 optional ONNX tests ignored), cargo fmt/check, canonical owner self-tests and previously implemented source-verified browser controls.
- **GPU runtime caveat:** Opt-in real CUDA integration smoke was actually attempted on the connected RTX 5070 Ti laptop/WSL and FAILED because Linux libonnxruntime.so is missing, despite CUDA shim availability. This is a missing deployment/runtime dependency, not a verified end-to-end successful GPU reel. The runner fails closed and does not fall back to stale model outputs or manufacture a successful MP4. Installing/provisioning matching ONNX Runtime/CUDA and a successful representative end-to-end test remain REQUIRED for Windows/WSL packaging (stage82) and benchmark acceptance (stage84). Do not switch production on the strength of these code-level checks.

Next planned stage 79/85: independent Quick Reel client file selection, cancel/progress/reconnect and verified MP4 download. No stage79 code was implemented during stage78.
## 79. Quick Reel: выбор файла, отмена, прогресс и скачивание — 2026-11-09

- Added a complete independent Quick Reel browser tab in rust/volley-app/static/studio.html, studio_tabs.js, studio.css and a small source-isolated controller studio_quick_reel.js. It works without a selected project, tracks up to 30 videos, requires the selected 10–600 s music, starts fresh stage78 CUDA+CPU+montage and shows explicit four stages (preparation / two independent GPU model scans / CPU / FFmpeg), per-video progress, cancellation, persistent quick status in the left sidebar and a verified MP4 download link. On page reload the client calls the read-only GET Quick Reel status endpoint and reattaches only to the latest existing run; it never accidentally re-starts inference. A single browser busy state guards concurrent picker, upload, start and cancel requests. The existing cross-process single-flight lock on the Rust backend protects against independent clients/servers.
- Click/Enter/Space on the dropzone invokes POST /api/quick-reel/native/pick, opening the actual Windows System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog, with multiselect, via Windows PowerShell STA. Calls from WSL resolve selected Windows absolute paths using wslpath -u; Windows-native Rust uses absolute Windows paths directly. Fixed PowerShell script is encoded to UTF-16LE base64 for -EncodedCommand to prevent Windows/WSL shell-quoting and Cyrillic pathname corruption. Path selection returns only random source UUID tokens and display filenames; original files are NOT copied. User cancellation returns cancelled:true without discarding earlier selections. The PowerShell program contains NO user-injected commands/paths, and all paths are canonicalized, stat-verified, and probed as playable videos by the existing Rust Quick Reel source path code.
- Browser drag-and-drop or the explicit browser file picker cannot reveal an original absolute path by design. They upload the selected video via streamed multipart POST /api/quick-reel/native/upload to a UUID-named file solely in the isolated Quick Reel uploads root (2 GiB/file hard bound; advise desktop picker for large originals), ffprobe-validate the video and return its UUID source token. Original user-selected display names are persisted separately from the safe on-disk UUID name. POST /api/quick-reel/native/release clears only explicitly selected tokens: dropped copies are removed iff located in the isolated uploads directory with generated prefix and no active local or cross-process Quick Reel lock; any linked original is never deleted.
- User-selected soundtrack is uploaded via the existing isolated Rust montage audio upload path. The client only sends selection_ids and audio_file to POST Quick Reel start, never absolute user paths or project-specific inference IDs. Status polling safely reflects queued/running/failed/cancelled/interrupted/complete without fabricated MP4 availability; cancellation propagates to native GPU/FFmpeg via the existing stage78 watch channel. A stable left-sidebar progress label keeps current stage visible while visiting other Studio tabs.
- GET /api/quick-reel/native/media?id=<run UUID> uses the existing canonical Rust MP4 byte-range streamer with Content-Disposition attachment, no-store and nosniff. Before opening the fixed quick_reel.mp4 path it rejects invalid run IDs, unsafe run/receipt/file symlinks, non-complete status, any result not marked fresh GPU/CPU, mismatched run or MP4 receipt, missing verified original clip/AAC quality evidence and missing/size-mismatched MP4. No caller-supplied path is opened, no download is exposed during incomplete or cancelled jobs. The client also hides download unless the completed response has an explicit original/audio verification receipt.
- New browser contract scripts/ci/test_native_quick_reel_ui.mjs verifies native picker flow (including cancellation preserving earlier files), sequential drag-and-drop upload, oversized-file rejection, explicit cleanup, fresh-run request without project data, music upload, four-stage GPU progress, single cancellation, reattachment after reload, status timer cleanup, and output evidence rejection. Native Rust tests drive a REAL Axum loopback HTTP server with synthetic FFmpeg-generated MP4 to exercise both link-only selection and multipart streaming, verify zero project pollution and exact temporary-copy cleanup; additional tests verify 206/Range download headers/bytes, inconsistent/partial receipts, source protection, and UTF-16LE PowerShell encoding. Actual native Windows PowerShell Forms interop and the OpenFileDialog script syntax were verified on the connected WSL/Windows host WITHOUT opening a GUI dialog. The interactive native picker and production CUDA end-to-end Quick Reel remain untested in this run.
- Verified stage79: 215 offline Rust tests passed (2 optional ONNX integration cases ignored), cargo fmt/check, browser contract and canonical source/code ownership/roadmap self-tests. Stage78 GPU dependency warning remains: WSL cannot complete native ONNX CUDA until the matching libonnxruntime.so and CUDA runtime stack are provisioned; do NOT call this full production-ready Quick Reel or start Python as a fallback. Stage80 is legacy projects reopening/archive only, stage81 is full WASM/RU-EN UI replacement, stage82 is Windows/WSL/Docker runtime packaging.

## 80. Совместимость старых проектов и безопасная очистка/архивация — 2026-11-09

- Added a read-only Rust compatibility adapter at rust/volley-app/src/legacy_projects.rs. GET /api/project-legacy/native/catalog and /inspect open the real historical repository_root/results/projects/projects.json and per-project project.json using strict 12-hex project IDs, bounded file sizes, symlink/traversal refusal, matching registry/detail names and video names, and bounded legacy run-directory inventory. This is READING historical metadata only; old analysis run names are NOT native Rust GPU/CPU replay IDs and are NOT silently imported as re-runnable model results.
- POST /api/project-legacy/native/import requires an explicit project ID and writes a small immutable-provenance metadata snapshot to the isolated Rust data_root/projects/<same legacy id>/project.json plus an isolated registry entry. Historical name, video list, selection ranges, original source_paths, athlete IDs and unknown Python project keys are preserved. No raw old analysis cache, original recording, global player album, prepared media or Python files under repository_root/results/ are ever copied, renamed, modified or removed. Original source_paths are retained as recorded rather than guessing a same-named substitute; invalid, missing or zero-byte original video files are explicitly marked unavailable, and no CPU/GPU reanalysis is inferred from legacy folders. Re-import of the same ID is refused, and an archived Rust copy must be restored instead of importing a conflicting duplicate. The import retains the original 12-character ID for future manual re-linking.
- Important **actual user legacy data observation**: the live historical registry contains a real old project 391314fa0653, named 06.11.2026, with two saved videos and historical run metadata. Both files currently present at repository_root/project/<filename> are **0 bytes**, and saved original source paths refer to missing Downloads media. A metadata-only reopen/inspection is possible and was TESTED; media playback, replaying old GPU/CPU results and successfully rerunning an inference on these missing files are NOT possible or claimed. Users must restore authentic source video before a later explicit source reconciliation; existing cached analyses are never relabeled as belonging to a different video.
- Extended the already recoverable POST /api/project-archive/native with GET /api/project-archive/native for a bounded, read-only Rust .trash inventory and POST /api/project-archive/native/restore for same-filesystem recovery with strict archive UUID format, symlink and manifest checks, per-project and registry locks, no overwrite of an active project and rollback on registry-write failure. Before archiving, isolated Rust projects without their own project.json get an isolated registry snapshot so the archived copy can be restored; invalid/mismatched or oversized project metadata refuses the move. The actual legacy results tree, original media, shared prepared cache and globally stored player photos are OUTSIDE this archived namespace, and only the isolated project folder is moved under projects/.trash. Nothing in the new paths implements permanent deletion.
- The separate Rust Studio browser bridge now exposes "Старые проекты и архив" in rust/volley-app/static/studio.html and studio_legacy.js (temporary JavaScript UI until stage81 WASM replacement). It displays old saved project names, IDs, legacy run labels and missing original videos; allows explicit metadata import and opening the registered Rust copy; and offers one-click reversible archive/restore for isolated Rust projects only. Existing project/rally view state is refreshed via a scoped browser event after changes. No old filesystem paths are sent to the frontend and all server-derived labels are rendered through textContent, not innerHTML.
- Hardened common Rust project registry handling to reject a symlink/oversized projects.json or symlinked registry lock/root on write, while still preserving unknown legacy project keys. A zero-byte source file does not pass availability or original-video validation. All imported source availability remains fail-closed; historical cache metadata is inventoried, not used to create native analysis authority.
- Added sandbox tests with a realistic old registry/ID/ranges, zero-byte video, and historical run folders proving the import preserves old fields and original file bytes, duplicate import refuses, malformed registry/detail mismatch refuses without creating a Rust project and symlinked legacy registry cannot escape the repository. The archive test now verifies the full archive -> restore round trip with unchanged project-owned saved analysis and global photo/prepared data, no permanent delete, and invalid archive ID rejection. The UI browser contract verifies missing-media notices, explicit import/open, isolated archive, restoration and no DELETE request.

Production source video files were never touched. Real playable legacy reanalysis remains blocked by absent original video bytes; this does not count as a full old-match GPU/CPU/video acceptance.

Browser mock tests for legacy import/archive/open/restore, independent Quick Reel, reviewed attacks and per-player statistics passed; Axum in-process HTTP tests served the new scripts and read-only API endpoints; Rust ownership checks passed with 19 canonical owners and 53 guarded routes (8 separate source-scoped sidecars), roadmap negative self-tests passed at 80/85. ## 81. Полноценный Rust/WASM интерфейс с RU/EN и настройками — 2026-11-09

A per-tab unsigned load generation invalidates stale responses when the user navigates between tabs. The WASM binary rust/volley-app/static/volley_web.wasm was freshly compiled with cargo build -p volley-web --target wasm32-unknown-unknown --release --offline and is served from Rust via GET /studio/rust/selection.wasm. No JS WASM fake or Python runtime is involved.

A mutation-aware same-origin DOM adapter updates only changed subtrees to avoid rescanning thousands of player cards on every GPU/CPU progress message. Switching back from EN to RU restores the original Russian copy; user media/filenames are not translated or mutated. Dynamic strings returned by native backend error handlers are not exhaustively localized yet.

These browser-only preferences persist across page reload in a versioned localStorage record with fail-closed type/range validation via WASM; storage-unavailable private mode works with volatile preferences. The light palette does NOT invert original video, photos or rendered match pixels.
